---
name: compensatory-router
description: "Meta-skill that routes any incoming task to the right compensatory skill pack (deep reasoning / context budget / verification / long-horizon execution) so a flash-tier model behaves like a frontier one without being told each time. Use on EVERY substantial task: pick exactly one primary skill + optional supports, and state the choice in one line before working."
---

# Compensatory Router

The pack exists to make strong-model *habits* automatic. At the start of any
substantial task, pick the primary compensator and say it in one line. Then
work. This is the difference between "has skills" and "uses skills".

## Proactive stack (15-skill pipeline — fires automatically)

**STATE -1 — MANDATORY REASONING GATE on EVERY request (user rule: 🧠 التفكير
والاستدلال شغّال مع أي طلب، مش بس المهام المعقدة):** load `frontier-deep-reasoner`
FIRST on any request, no exceptions, and apply its decomposition ladder to the
incoming request BEFORE anything else (before clarify, before omni, before any
task-specific row): Problem → Constraints → Steps → Verify → Output. This is
THE one skill guaranteed on every request, and it always runs first. The rest
of the stack below routes after this gate; the reasoning ladder then rides
along as the reasoning backbone of whatever task row fires.

**STATE -2 — MANDATORY VIDEO-SKILL-PACK BASELINE on EVERY request (user rule:
المهارات ديه تشتغل بشكل أساسي مع أي طلب — no exceptions):** the 5 video skills
fire as a BASELINE layer on every request, composing with the task-specific row
below, not replacing it:
- `using-superpowers` (obra pack orchestrator): before touching the project,
  run the plan→review discipline — `writing-plans`/`executing-plans` structure
  the work, `verification-before-completion` gates delivery, and
  `test-driven-development` guards code changes. For any request whose outcome
  is code/UI, route the production step through `impeccable` (frontend
  architecture + UI quality). For any multi-session or 'my project' work, load
  `remembering-conversations` so the user never re-explains their project.
  `wizard` observes how the user works and evolves skills in the background.
- The task-specific row below still governs the actual HOW; this baseline only
  adds plan-first, verify-before-completion, and quality discipline around it.

**STATE -2b — MANDATORY AUTOMATIC SKILL DISCOVERY on EVERY request (user rule:
find-skills تشتغل أوتوماتيك في الخلفية من غير ما ترجعلي):** while ANY task runs,
`find-skills` resolves the task's domain against the LOCAL LIBRARY first —
scan `memory/skills-library.md` (the generated one-line index of every
installed skill, grouped by this router's registry buckets) for an installed
skill that already covers the task; if found, adopt it directly and stop. Only
when NO installed skill fits does it check the background for a better
off-the-shelf skill (skills.sh / `npx skills find`). If a match with real
traction (install count 1K+, trusted owners) is found, INSTALL it directly into
`.opencode/skills/` (re-check frontmatter), REGISTER it in this router via
`venv/bin/python scripts/router_register.py <name>` (adds a routing row marked
`(Auto-registered)` + a registry entry + bumps the total + auto-refreshes
`memory/skills-library.md`), adopt it for the current task, and report the
install in the final answer. If NO installable match exists, CREATE the skill
in-house: write `.opencode/skills/<slug>/SKILL.md`, run
`venv/bin/python scripts/build_gates_pipeline.py .opencode/skills/<slug>/SKILL.md --no-hitl`
until VERDICT READY_FOR_DEPLOYMENT (exit 0), then `router_register.py <slug>`
(which also adds its library entry). DO NOT pause the task to ask the user for
confirmation and DO NOT come back with 'should I install X?'. Exceptions that
DO stop and ask: paid/high-risk (`Med/High` Socket/Snyk flagged) or huge packs
(>5 skills) — those get a one-line note, not a blocking question.

**STATE -2c — MANDATORY GATE-FIRST-PASS BASELINE before EVERY n8n workflow /
AI agent build (user rule: أي workflow أو agent تبنيه لازم يعدي على البوابات من
أول مرة — build it right the first time):** `gate-first-pass-builder` fires
before any node is placed on ANY build/edit/creation of a workflow, agent,
automation, or n8n JSON. It reads the LIVE avoid-list from the accumulated
rejection history (`venv/bin/python scripts/gate_first_pass_avoidlist.py
--top 10` reading `memory/n8n_error_patterns.json`) so the design is blocked
from the real gate failures (schema cache preflight first, pinned data +
dry-run evidence, Error Trigger / continueOnFail, sub-workflow split for large
graphs, agent wiring: ai_languageModel connected + allowed_tools declared +
maxIterations ceiling + systemMessage, webhook auth when writing, credentials
never inline, integer typeVersion, no orphaned/dangling nodes, RAG
embeddings/store/splitter wiring), then builds incrementally and runs
`build_gates_pipeline.py --schema-cache memory/n8n_schema_cache.json` until
VERDICT READY_FOR_DEPLOYMENT (exit 0). A gate rejection on a rule-compliant
build is reported as a possible false positive — never worked around by
reshaping the workflow. Composes with STATE -2 video baseline, STATE 0 clarify,
and the n8n task rows below (incremental-generation, build-gates-pipeline,
n8n-schema-guardrail, automation-known-issues-compass).

**STATE 0 — MANDATORY discovery loop on ANY request (user rule, applies to
everything):** `clarify-before-execute` FIRST — if the request is not fully
specified, SEARCH first (web/skills.sh/codebase/memory) to ground it, then
COME BACK and ask the user the load-bearing questions (goal, exact
deliverable, audience, constraints, done-when) in ONE compact round with
defaults, get the confirmation contract, and only then proceed to the omni
pipeline below. Never build on silent assumptions; never ask twice about
what search/memory already answers.

**MANDATORY for EVERY request (user rule, applies to everything):** run
`omni-request-orchestrator` FIRST on any task — extract purpose + target
audience → analyze their psychology with LIVE 2025+ web research
(`audience-psychology-analyst` + `web-search`/`deep-research`) → pick the
matching marketing levers (`influence-psychology`/`conversion-psychology`/
`persuasion-principles`/`marketing-psychology`) → hand the psychological
brief to the production skill for THAT task (`video-edit`, `copywriting`,
`social-media`, n8n stack, etc.) → verify output against the psychology.
Psychology is NEVER "unrelated" — even code/n8n tasks get the audience pass.
**If unsure which skills apply (novel/unusual task): fire ALL the relevant
packs together** — psychology + research + quality/security + production —
and synthesize; never leave a skill idle because it "looks unrelated"
(user rule: شغل كل المهارات مع بعض حتى لو شايف إن ملهمش لزمة).
Then continue with the task-specific row below.

For **any system build or refactor**, do NOT start coding from the raw prompt.
Expand first, decompose second, document last. This is the 30% a frontier
model does implicitly:

| Task type | Mandatory skill stack | Resulting behavior |
|---|---|---|
| Any build / refactor (New Feature / New System) | `proactive-spec-expander` + `zero-trust-modular-decomposer` + `long-horizon-executor` | expands the request proactively (security/lockdown/rate-limit/edge cases) → splits code into 7+ files (core / security / resilience / audit) → executes with a tight plan |
| Code review / architecture (Review / Architecture) | `tradeoff-and-postmortem-documenter` + `evidence-over-memory` | documents decisions and trade-offs in `KNOWN_ISSUES.md` grounded in live evidence |
| Web automation / login / scrape / forms (Web Automation) | `persistent-browser-automation` + `browser-automation` + `stealth-browser-evasion` + `hitl-captcha-auth-handler` | runs in stealth mode (bypass WAF anti-bot) → pauses for human only when CAPTCHA/2FA appears → resumes and persists the session |
| Logging into MY accounts / my logged-in sessions (Site Login) | `site-login-session-registry` + `persistent-browser-automation` + `hitl-captcha-auth-handler` | looks up the saved profile → reuses the logged-in session → pauses for human only on 2FA/CAPTCHA → renews and re-persists the session |
| Complex algorithmic / mathematical / proof task (Hard Math) | `algorithmic-math-reasoner` (5 gates) + `frontier-deep-reasoner` (+ `off-by-one-boundary-guard` when the answer is a COUNT) | restates formally → strawman+attack → invariant/amortized proof → brute-force randomized cross-check → paper trace ; claims are certificates, not guesses |
| Counting / 'how many' / open-closed intervals / ranges (Boundary counting) | `off-by-one-boundary-guard` (5 gates) + `algorithmic-math-reasoner` (+ `unit-test-boundary-conditions` when the boundary risk is in CODE: loops, ranges, edge tests) | boundary inventory → fence-post formula → endpoint plug-in test → encoding-independent enumeration → second-method parity ; kills the ±1 class (AIME 2025 I-18: 149 not 150) |
| Hard problem that beat one pass / needs frontier certainty (Test-time scaling) | `test-time-compute-scaling` + `algorithmic-math-reasoner` (+ `elite-verifier-delegation` if a stronger model exists) | gates on difficulty → generates 3–5 parallel candidates → selects via verifier (execution / brute-force / self-consistency vote) → escalates ties honestly |
| Watching a video/social reel the page-fetcher can't read (Media) | `media-downloader-extractor` + `audio-whisper-transcriber` + `obsidian-second-brain-vault` (+ `desktop-gui-controller` if human auth/2FA needed) | pulls audio with yt-dlp (anon → cookies fallback) → transcribes locally with faster-whisper → writes raw transcript into Vault/01_Raw_Inbox + a synthesized note in Vault/02_Structured_Knowledge → summarize |
| 'Second opinion / certify with a stronger model' (Elite verify) | `elite-verifier-delegation` (+ `multi-agent-consensus-engine` for cheap judges) | generates cheap here → hands ONLY the final answer to the strongest available judge → adversarial verdict (correct/incorrect/caveats) → reconcile = fix + re-submit changed part |
| 'Compare yourself to Fable / run a benchmark' (Benchmark) | `self-benchmark-runner` + `algorithmic-math-reasoner` + `evidence-over-memory` (+ `off-by-one-boundary-guard` for every counting problem in the set) | picks a free public benchmark with published frontier scores → declares sample BEFORE seeing key → locks answer key → solves under skill stack → grades mechanically → reports honest score side-by-side |
| 'Find a skill for X / is there a skill for X' (Skill discovery) | `find-skills` (+ `fable-5-playbook` when the ask is 'behave like a frontier model') | **LIBRARY FIRST**: scan `memory/skills-library.md` (one-line index of every installed skill, grouped by this router's registry buckets; open `memory/skills-docs/<name>.md` for the full doc of any hit) — if an installed skill already covers the task, adopt it and STOP. Only when none fits: search the open agent-skills ecosystem (skills.sh / `npx skills find`) → verify quality (install count 1K+, trusted owners) → installs into `.opencode/skills/` → re-checks frontmatter → **registers in the router via `scripts/router_register.py`** (routing row + registry entry + auto-refreshes the library). **User rule: runs AUTOMATICALLY in the background on EVERY request (STATE -2b above) — installs silently, never pauses to ask; only paid/high-risk/huge packs get a one-line note. If no installable skill exists → CREATE it in-house and pass it through `build_gates_pipeline.py` (READY_FOR_DEPLOYMENT) then `router_register.py`** |
| 'Skills from the viral "5 Claude skills" video (Abdallah Khraisat)' (Video skill pack) | `using-superpowers` (obra/superpowers pack: brainstorming / writing-plans / executing-plans / test-driven-development / systematic-debugging / requesting-code-review / receiving-code-review / subagent-driven-development / dispatching-parallel-agents / using-git-worktrees / finishing-a-development-branch / verification-before-completion / writing-skills) + `remembering-conversations` (episodic memory) + `impeccable` (frontend architecture/UI quality) + `wizard` (learns how the user works, improves skills in the background) | the 5 skills from the viral video: (1) find-skills already installed, (2) **superpowers** — write a plan + review BEFORE touching the project (planning/review discipline), (3) **claude-memory** = `remembering-conversations` — memory between sessions so the user never re-explains their project, (4) **impeccable** — architecture-grade frontend design/UI, (5) **wizard** — learns the user's working style and evolves skills in the background. **User rule: this pack is now the MANDATORY baseline on EVERY request (STATE -2 above) — plan-first, verify-before-completion, quality discipline always on** |

| 'Office/CRM/sales/dev/IT automation x' (Office Automation) | `<x>-automation` — the full installed pack: `sheets-automation`, `excel-automation`, `airtable-automation`, `notion-automation`, `mailchimp-automation`, `crm-automation`, `email-marketing`, `hubspot`, `jira-automation`, `linear-automation`, `trello-automation`, `asana-automation`, `monday.com-automation` (available globally as Monday.com Automation), `clickup-automation`, `calendar-automation`, `docusign-automation`, `invoice-automation`, `quickbooks-automation`, `pipedrive-automation`, `shopify-automation`, `woocommerce-automation`, `linkedin-automation`, `twitter-x-automation`, `youtube-automation`, `spotify-automation`, `whatsapp-automation`, `twilio-sms-automation`, `microsoft-teams-automation`, `intercom-automation`, `zendesk-automation`, `home-assistant-automation`, `weather-automation`, `devops-automation`, `ansible-automation`, `hr-automation`, `webhook-automation`, `transcription-automation`, `podcast-automation`, `obsidian-automation`, `security-monitoring` | full claude-office-skills + SaaS automation pack installed: 35+ per-tool skills, wired by matching the tool name in the request to its `<tool>-automation` skill |
| Long session / nearing context limit (Context) | `progressive-context-compressor` + `context-budget-governor` + `long-term-memory-retriever` | keeps a rolling capsule, drops chatter but never identifiers/decisions/security, rehydrates detail on demand |
| Session ending / 'save what we learned' (Consolidate) | `durable-experience-consolidator` | distills principles/facts (with evidence)/failures+root-cause/recipes → appends to memory → re-encodes `.bin` → verifies MATCH |
| Visual / rendered / GUI claim (Visual proof) | `visual-context-verifier` + `desktop-gui-controller` | captures real pixels → reads and compares → reports the seen, not the assumption |
| Using my real device / desktop / windows (Device GUI) | `desktop-gui-controller` (+ `site-login-session-registry` if the goal is a site) | drives the real X11 desktop (windows, keyboard, mouse, screenshots, clipboard) with before/after evidence and safety gates |
| Coding / refactoring (Claude Coworker Stack) | `subagent-task-delegator` + `surgical-diff-patch-editor` + `terminal-bash-executor-governor` + `autonomous-git-coworker` | receives the request → splits it into sub-agents → edits via surgical diffs → runs commands and Tests in the terminal → pushes changes via Git cleanly |
| Hard coding / algorithms / debugging (Elite coding) | `algorithm-design` + `code-debugging` + `experiment-code` + `paper-to-code` (+ `atomic-decomposition`) | designs algorithms properly → debugs systematically → verifies by running experiments → ports paper results to code |
| Math / symbolic / equations (Elite math) | `math-reasoning` + `symbolic-equation` (+ `algorithmic-math-reasoner`) | solves math and symbolic problems with rigorous multi-path reasoning + brute-force cross-checks |

| Deep logic / critical thinking / reasoning (Elite logic) | `critical-thinking-logical-reasoning` + `thought-based-reasoning` (+ `frontier-deep-reasoner`) | forces assumption-checking, fallacy detection, and structured thought chains before conclusions |

| 'Big open-ended build in ONE coherent pass / from scratch' (Single-pass frontier) | `single-pass-frontier-emulator` (+ `proactive-spec-expander` + `frontier-deep-reasoner` inside the pass) | whole-picture contract first → FULL artifact in one response → internal attack rounds (integration/edges/self-falsify) → one verification command → green before report — delegation only for EXECUTION, never for design |

| 'Open-ended creative problem / novel idea / something never tried' (Emergent reasoning) | `lateral-thinking` pack (analogy/inversion/lateral/random-stimulus/scamper/six-hats/provocation) + `cc-thinking-skills` pack (24: first-principles, thought-experiment, ooda, pre-mortem, probabilistic, red-team, systems, theory-of-constraints, second-order, socratic...) + `first-principles` + `creative-problem-solver`/`concept-fan`/`worst-idea` (+ `llm-council`, `multi-agent-consensus-engine`) | provoke divergent options first (random stimuli, inversions, worst idea as spark) → converge via structured thinking frames (pre-mortem, red-team, steel-man, probabilistic, thought experiment) → council/consensus verdict → honest confidence |
| 'Novel idea / breakthrough thinking that must BEAT a frontier model's instinct' (Emergent edge — live-world + falsified) | `emergent-reasoning-edge` (PRIMARY) + lateral pack + `llm-council` (+ `parallel-web-search`/`deep-research` for LIVE evidence, + `adversarial-self-falsifier`, + `evidence-over-memory`) | 6 gates: reframe N ways (inversion/analogy/random-stimulus/scamper) → divergent generation (4-6 mechanisms + worst-idea spark) → LIVE evidence injection (web/firecrawl/apify, cited — Claude's instinct is frozen at cutoff) → mechanical falsification rounds (red-team, worst-idea, pre-mortem, council vote; survivors ≤2) → synthesis with survival-calibrated confidence → iteration log (`memory/emergent-idea-log.md`) so ideas compound across sessions |
| 'Whole repo in mind across sessions / large codebase depth' (Codebase mind) | `codebase-mind-persistence` (+ `ast-codebase-graph-navigator` to build; + `long-context-sharding-engine` for bulk reads) | builds persistent ≤2K-token mind map once (symbols/modules/deps/tests) → every later session loads it → targeted rehydration via pointers → surgical maintenance, stale-marked on refactors |

| 'Use egress-firewall: "Runtime egress firewall skill (private/metadata IP walls, all-answers DNS check, domain …' (Auto-registered) | `egress-firewall` | "Runtime egress firewall skill (private/metadata IP walls, all-answers DNS check, domain allow-list, scheme/port policy). Use before any outbound fetch, when a URL comes from untrusted input, or when public internet needs explicit opt-in. Trigger phrases: 'check this URL', 'egress policy', 'SSRF runtime guard', 'فحص الوجهة'." |
| 'Use secrets-vault: "Short-lived secrets vault skill (scoped leases, single-use option, revoke/rotate, access …' (Auto-registered) | `secrets-vault` | "Short-lived secrets vault skill (scoped leases, single-use option, revoke/rotate, access log). Use when an agent needs a credential for one job, when a lease must die after use or on incident, or when credential access needs an audit trail. Trigger phrases: 'issue a lease', 'short-lived credential', 'revoke lease', 'تصريح مؤقت'." |
| 'Use supply-chain-security: "Supply-chain security skill (Python SBOM, lockfile verify, skill-hash verify, honest …' (Auto-registered) | `supply-chain-security` | "Supply-chain security skill (Python SBOM, lockfile verify, skill-hash verify, honest vuln-scan, artifact signing). Use when onboarding dependencies, verifying installed skills against the lockfile, or signing a build artifact. Trigger phrases: 'SBOM', 'verify lockfile', 'skill hash check', 'سلسلة التوريد'." |
| 'Use skill-trust-registry: "Skill trust registry skill (sealed identity/version/hash/permissions/risk store, load …' (Auto-registered) | `skill-trust-registry` | "Skill trust registry skill (sealed identity/version/hash/permissions/risk store, load verdicts ok/review/unknown/tamper). Use when loading any skill, onboarding a new one, or triaging a modified skill file. Trigger phrases: 'trust this skill', 'skill verdict', 'registry check', 'سجل الثقة'." |
## Decision table

| Task shape | Primary skill |
|---|---|
| One complex answer / bug / design / algorithm | `frontier-deep-reasoner` |
| Long session, big repo, many files, big reads | `context-budget-governor` |
| Any factual/API/version/path/number claim | `evidence-over-memory` |
| 3+ interdependent code steps (build/refactor/migrate) | `long-horizon-executor` |
| Long AND complex AND multi-step | `long-horizon-executor` + `context-budget-governor` (note file) |
| Anything that asserts a fact while reasoning | `evidence-over-memory` rides along |

## Super-Agent add-on routing

| Trigger | Skill |
|---|---|
| "Is my solution actually correct / harden this" | `adversarial-self-falsifier` (after drafting) |
| Architecture / security / big refactor decisions | `multi-agent-consensus-engine` |
| Writing or changing any code | `tdd-sandbox-proof-engine` |
| Navigating an unknown/large codebase | `ast-codebase-graph-navigator` |
| Anything failed (test, crash, wrong output) | `root-cause-post-mortem-analyzer` |
| Any live site/login/scrape/form/download task | `persistent-browser-automation` |

Stacking rules: the add-on skills COMPOSE with the base ones. Example routing
for a large refactor: `long-horizon-executor` (plan/checkpoints) +
`ast-codebase-graph-navigator` (navigate) + `multi-agent-consensus-engine`
(review the plan) + `tdd-sandbox-proof-engine` (verify each step) +
`adversarial-self-falsifier` (attack the final result).

## Activation line (say it, do not skip it)
`[router] primary=..., support=...` — one line, then execute the chosen protocol.

## Anti-patterns (do not do)
- Do not recite several skills "in case" — one primary governs the flow.
- Do not skip the chosen protocol mid-task because it feels slow; the slowness
  is exactly the frontier-model behavior the user asked for.
- Do not apply `long-horizon-executor` to a one-step question.
